## Platforms <a id="platforms"></a>

| Id | Capability | What it does | Rive mechanism | Kind | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `platforms.android-bitmap` | Android bitmap capture | The runtime documents offscreen bitmap rendering for screenshots and video workflows. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/android/rendering-to-a-bitmap |
| `platforms.android-compose` | Android Compose runtime | The new Compose API is described as beta but production ready; an explicit worker owns file decoding, advancement and rendering. | `Rive`, `rememberRiveWorker` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/android/android |
| `platforms.android-renderers` | Android renderer choices | Compose uses the Rive Renderer; the legacy renderer choices differ by version and Skia was removed in 10.0.0. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/choose-a-renderer/overview |
| `platforms.android-views` | Android legacy Views runtime | The View/XML API uses RiveAnimationView and can be wrapped in Compose; new feature work targets the Compose API. | `RiveAnimationView` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/android/android |
| `platforms.angular` | Angular community wrapper | A community-maintained Angular wrapper exists; Rive does not maintain it. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/community-runtimes/angular |
| `platforms.apple-mask-simulator` | Apple mask restrictions | Layer masks require GPU Canvas on supported physical devices, macOS and Catalyst; simulators do not support them. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/feature-support |
| `platforms.apple-workers` | Apple Swift concurrency | Workers process runtime tasks off the main thread while the public API enforces MainActor access. | `Worker` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/apple/apple |
| `platforms.canvas-advanced` | @rive-app/canvas-advanced | A separate package exposes the low-level module for host-owned rendering and playback. | `@rive-app/canvas-advanced` | runtime | rive-wasm/js/npm/canvas_advanced/package.json:2@ada7b9f35f951ff491f65f5ffc74a3ed3c582203 |

| `platforms.canvas-advanced-single` | @rive-app/canvas-advanced-single | A separate package exposes the low-level module for host-owned rendering and playback. | `@rive-app/canvas-advanced-single` | runtime | rive-wasm/js/npm/canvas_advanced_single/package.json:2@ada7b9f35f951ff491f65f5ffc74a3ed3c582203 |
| `platforms.canvas-feathering` | Canvas feathering differences | Full Canvas supports feathering with gradient banding limitations; Canvas Lite does not. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.compose-multiplatform` | Compose Multiplatform community wrapper | The community wrapper combines Apple, Android and Canvas web runtimes behind one API. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/community-runtimes/rive-cmp |
| `platforms.cpp` | C++ host runtime | Embed the runtime in desktop or native applications and own its rendering and advancement loop. | `File`, `ArtboardInstance`, `StateMachineInstance` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/overview |
| `platforms.cpp-command-queue` | C++ threaded command queue | CommandQueue and CommandServer separate client commands from worker-owned runtime objects. | `CommandQueue`, `CommandServer` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/command-queue |
| `platforms.cpp-d3d11` | C++ Direct3D 11 | Use the D3D11 context factory and a suitable render target. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.cpp-d3d12` | C++ Direct3D 12 | Supply recording command lists and frame fence progress. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.cpp-metal` | C++ Metal | Supply a Metal command buffer; the host owns presentation. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.cpp-opengl` | C++ OpenGL / GLES | Wrap a texture or framebuffer and select a driver-supported rendering path. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.cpp-vulkan` | C++ Vulkan | Supply a recording command buffer and safe frame numbers; frame splitting is Vulkan-specific. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.cpp-webgl2` | C++ C++ WebGL2 build | Build the GL backend with Emscripten for WebGL2. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/cpp/renderers |
| `platforms.csharp` | C# UWP community runtime | An experimental Windows Community Toolkit package offers UWP integration; APIs may change. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/community-runtimes/c-sharp |
| `platforms.defold` | Defold integration | Defold maintains the integration and uses Rive's native renderer; Rive docs do not specify a full per-feature support matrix. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/game-runtimes/defold |
| `platforms.feature-negotiation` | Feature-specific compatibility | Renderer and runtime version jointly determine support; layer masks require GPU Canvas across runtimes. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/feature-support |

| `platforms.web-canvas` | Web Canvas2D | The full Canvas package draws through Canvas2D; clockwise winding is approximated by nonzero winding. | `@rive-app/canvas` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.web-canvas-lite` | Web Canvas Lite | The smaller Canvas package omits text, layout, audio, scripting and feathering; affected content is absent. | `@rive-app/canvas-lite` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.web-canvas-single` | Web embedded WASM build | Canvas Single embeds WASM in JavaScript, avoiding a separate WASM request. | `@rive-app/canvas-single` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.web-layer-masks` | Web layer-mask backend | Layer masks require GPU Canvas on WebGL2 and are unavailable in Canvas, Canvas Lite and legacy WebGL. |  | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/feature-support |
| `platforms.web-webgl-legacy` | Legacy WebGL package | The old WebGL package is deprecated and stops receiving updates after 2.37.0. | `@rive-app/webgl` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.web-webgl2` | Web WebGL2 | The WebGL2 package uses Rive's renderer; browser context limits can constrain many independent players. | `@rive-app/webgl2` | runtime | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/web/canvas-vs-webgl |
| `platforms.webgl2-advanced` | @rive-app/webgl2-advanced | A separate package exposes the low-level module for host-owned rendering and playback. | `@rive-app/webgl2-advanced` | runtime | rive-wasm/js/npm/webgl2_advanced/package.json:2@ada7b9f35f951ff491f65f5ffc74a3ed3c582203 |
| `platforms.webgl2-threads` | @rive-app/webgl2-threads | A separate package exposes the threaded WebGL2 build; browser thread prerequisites still apply. | `@rive-app/webgl2-threads` | runtime | rive-wasm/js/npm/webgl2_threads/package.json:2@ada7b9f35f951ff491f65f5ffc74a3ed3c582203 |

## Tooling <a id="tooling"></a>

| Id | Capability | What it does | Rive mechanism | Kind | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tooling.account-list` | Account project browser | List projects, folders, files and recent activity using tables or JSON. | `rive ls` | tooling | rive ls --help |
| `tooling.analytics-policy` | Usage analytics setting | Query or toggle the CLI usage analytics setting. | `rive analytics` | tooling | rive analytics --help |

| `tooling.binary-rive-io` | Rive binary import/export | Rive uses .riv runtime documents; implementing compatibility IO would violate Anima's independence boundary. |  | tooling | https://rive.app/docs/runtimes/advanced-topic/format |
| `tooling.cli-uninstall` | Cached version removal | Remove a selected or unused cached CLI installation. | `rive uninstall` | tooling | rive uninstall --help |
| `tooling.cli-update` | CLI updates | Install and select the newest release on the selected release stream. | `rive update` | tooling | rive update --help |
| `tooling.cli-version-switch` | CLI version selection | Select cached or published versions or follow a named release stream. | `rive switch` | tooling | rive switch --help |
| `tooling.collaborative-editing` | Concurrent editing | Multiple collaborators can work in one file while history tracks their changes. |  | tooling | https://rive.app/docs/editor/fundamentals/revision-history |
| `tooling.data-breakpoints` | Data write breakpoints | Pause on property writes from scripts, listeners or bindings and identify the responsible source. | `rive --debug` | tooling | rive docs debugging |
| `tooling.data-debugger` | Live data inspection and editing | Inspect nested models/lists, edit typed values and fire triggers during preview or at a breakpoint. | `rive --debug` | tooling | rive docs debugging |
| `tooling.debug-rebuild` | Preserve debugger session on rebuild | Reinstall breakpoints after source changes; rebuilds received during a stop wait for continuation. | `rive --debug` | tooling | rive docs debugging |
